- 5 TM segments × 20 residues = 100 residues in TM (57% of sequence)  
- 6 TM segments × 20 residues = 120 residues in TM (69% of sequence)
- 7 TM segments × 20 residues = 140 residues in TM (80% of sequence)

For multi-pass membrane proteins, typically about 50-70% of residues are in TM segments. This suggests either 4, 5, or possibly 6 TM segments.
